| Season | Team | Level | Batting<br>Batting - Park Adjusted Runs Above Average based on wOBA | Base Running<br>Base Running - Base running runs above average, includes SB or CS | Fielding<br>Fielding - Fielding Runs Above Average based on OAA (2016-Current), UZR (2002-2015), TZ (1871-2002) | Positional<br>Positional - Positional Adjustment set at +12.5 for C, +7.5 for SS, +2.5 for 2B/3B/CF, -7.5 for RF/LF, -12.5 for 1B, -17.5 for DH | divider | Offense<br>Offense - Batting and Base Running combined (above average) | Defense<br>Defense - Fielding and Positional Adjustment combined (above average) | divider | League<br>League - League adjustment to zero out wins above average | Replacement<br>Replacement - Replacement Runs set at 20 runs per 600 plate apperances | divider | RAR<br>RAR - Runs Above Replacement (Batting + Fielding + Base Running + Replacement + Positional) | WAR<br>WAR - Wins Above Replacement | Dollars<br>Dollars - WAR converted to a dollar scale based on what a player would make in free agency | Salary<br>Salary - The player's actual salary |
